
> prop-scout-api@1.0.0 start
> node server.js


⚾  Prop Scout API  →  http://localhost:3001
   /health              server status + cache
   /api/schedule        today's games + probable pitchers
   /api/lineups/:pk     confirmed batting order
   /api/players/:id/stats  season stats + splits
   /api/umpires/:pk     home plate umpire
   /api/arsenal/:id     pitcher pitch mix (Baseball Savant)
   /api/splits/:id      batter splits vs pitch type (Baseball Savant)
   /api/nrfi/:gamePk    first-inning scoring tendencies
   /api/bullpen/:id     bullpen fatigue + reliever usage

   /api/auth/login     POST — login, returns JWT
   /api/auth/me        GET  — current user (protected)
   /api/picks          user-scoped pick log CRUD
   /api/notes/:gamePk  user-scoped game notes
   /api/digest         user-scoped 7-day pick digest

   /api/admin/jobs/run GET  — admin trigger for snapshot jobs
